<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ms">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover">
<title>Joomla 6.2 ms-MY — Jadual Terjemahan</title>
<style>
:root{--bg:#f6f7f9;--card:#fff;--ink:#1c2430;--mute:#667085;--line:#e3e7ee;--acc:#0b6bcb;--accbg:#e8f1fc;--ms:#0a6b4b;--hl:#fff1a8;box-sizing:border-box;padding-top:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px);padding-bottom:env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px)}
@media (prefers-color-scheme:dark){:root:not([data-theme="light"]){--bg:#0f141b;--card:#171e28;--ink:#e6eaf0;--mute:#98a2b3;--line:#2a3441;--acc:#6fb1ff;--accbg:#18304d;--ms:#59d6a6;--hl:#6b5a00;color-scheme:dark}}
:root[data-theme="dark"]{--bg:#0f141b;--card:#171e28;--ink:#e6eaf0;--mute:#98a2b3;--line:#2a3441;--acc:#6fb1ff;--accbg:#18304d;--ms:#59d6a6;--hl:#6b5a00;color-scheme:dark}
*,*::before,*::after{box-sizing:inherit}
html{scroll-padding-top:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px)}
body{margin:0;background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink);font:15px/1.5 system-ui,-apple-system,"Segoe UI",Roboto,Arial,sans-serif}
.wrap{max-width:1200px;margin:0 auto;padding:20px 16px 48px}
h1{font-size:1.4rem;margin:0 0 4px}
.sub{color:var(--mute);margin:0 0 16px}
.about{margin:0 0 14px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;background:var(--card);padding:10px 14px}
.about summary{cursor:pointer;font-weight:600;color:var(--acc)}
.about ul{margin:10px 0 4px;padding-left:20px;color:var(--ink)}
.about li{margin:4px 0}
.about .en{color:var(--mute);font-size:.88rem;display:block}
.about a{color:var(--acc)}
.bar{position:sticky;top:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px);z-index:5;background:var(--bg);padding:10px 0;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px;align-items:center;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
input[type=search],select{font:inherit;color:var(--ink);background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;padding:9px 12px;min-width:0}
input[type=search]{flex:1 1 260px}
select{flex:0 1 240px}
.chip{display:inline-flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;background:var(--accbg);color:var(--acc);padding:6px 12px;border-radius:999px;font-weight:600;font-size:.85rem;white-space:nowrap}
.credit{margin:0 0 6px;font-size:.95rem}
.credit a{color:var(--acc)}
.dl{display:inline-block;text-decoration:none;color:#fff !important;font-weight:600;background:var(--acc);color:#fff;border:0;border-radius:8px;padding:6px 14px;cursor:pointer;margin-left:6px}
.tog{display:inline-flex;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;overflow:hidden}
.tog button{font:inherit;background:var(--card);color:var(--mute);border:0;padding:8px 12px;cursor:pointer}
.tog button[aria-pressed=true]{background:var(--accbg);color:var(--acc);font-weight:600}
.scroll{overflow-x:auto;margin-top:12px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;background:var(--card)}
table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;min-width:720px}
th{position:sticky;top:0;background:var(--card);text-align:left;font-size:.78rem;letter-spacing:.04em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mute);padding:10px 12px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
td{padding:10px 12px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);vertical-align:top;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
tr:last-child td{border-bottom:0}
td.k{width:24%;font:12px/1.4 ui-monospace,SFMono-Regular,Menlo,Consolas,monospace;color:var(--mute)}
td.k b{display:block;color:var(--acc);font-weight:600;margin-bottom:2px}
td.en{width:36%;color:var(--mute)}
td.ms{width:40%;color:var(--ms);font-weight:500}
mark{background:var(--hl);color:inherit;border-radius:3px;padding:0 1px}
.nl{opacity:.55;font-size:.85em}
.more{display:block;margin:16px auto 0;font:inherit;background:var(--acc);color:#fff;border:0;border-radius:8px;padding:10px 20px;cursor:pointer}
.empty{padding:32px;text-align:center;color:var(--mute)}
.hide-en .en,.hide-en th.en{display:none}
.hide-en td.ms{width:58%}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div class="wrap">
<h1>Joomla 6.2 &mdash; Bahasa Melayu (ms-MY)</h1>
<p class="credit">Diterjemah dengan Claude AI oleh <a href="https://t.me/joomlamalaysia" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">t.me/joomlamalaysia</a> <a class="dl" id="dl" href="#">Download Here</a></p>
<p class="sub">Jadual terjemahan: semua rentetan bahasa laman (site) berbanding teks asal Bahasa Inggeris (en-GB).</p>
<details class="about">
<summary>Tentang terjemahan ini / About this translation</summary>
<ul>
<li>Diterjemah dengan Claude AI oleh Saifuddin (<a href="https://t.me/joomlamalaysia" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">t.me/joomlamalaysia</a>), berdasarkan pek bahasa en-GB Joomla 6.2.0.<span class="en">Translated with Claude AI by Saifuddin, based on the Joomla 6.2.0 en-GB language pack.</span></li>
<li>Kira-kira 46% rentetan (1,157 daripada 2,499) diguna semula daripada pek ms-MY komuniti <a href="https://github.com/MUDOTMY/Joomla-ms-MY-Site" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">MUDOTMY</a> untuk Joomla 3.4 (GPL), hanya apabila teks Inggeris tidak berubah. Rentetan ini telah disemak dan diperbaiki, termasuk menyeragamkan istilah.<span class="en">About 46% of strings (1,157 of 2,499) were reused from the community ms-MY pack by MUDOTMY (Joomla 3.4, GPL) only where the English text was unchanged, then reviewed and corrected, including unified terminology.</span></li>
<li>Selebihnya (1,342 rentetan) diterjemah baharu.<span class="en">The remaining 1,342 strings are newly translated.</span></li>
<li>Belum disemak oleh penutur asli. Sila uji di laman staging dahulu.<span class="en">Not yet reviewed by a native speaker. Please test on a staging site first.</span></li>
<li>Lesen: GNU General Public License versi 2 atau lebih baharu.<span class="en">License: GNU General Public License version 2 or later.</span></li>
</ul>
</details>
<div class="bar">
<input type="search" id="q" placeholder="Cari kunci, English atau Melayu&hellip;" aria-label="Cari">
<select id="f" aria-label="Fail bahasa"></select>
<div class="tog" role="group" aria-label="Paparan"><button id="b1" aria-pressed="true">EN + MS</button><button id="b2" aria-pressed="false">MS sahaja</button></div>
<span class="chip" id="cnt"></span>
</div>
<div class="scroll"><table id="t"><thead><tr><th>Kunci / Fail</th><th class="en">English (en-GB)</th><th class="ms">Bahasa Melayu (ms-MY)</th></tr></thead><tbody id="tb"></tbody></table></div>
<div id="emp" class="empty" hidden>Tiada padanan ditemui.</div>
<button class="more" id="more" hidden>Papar lagi</button>
</div>

As a security measure, you must confirm that this is a valid request for your personal information from this website.\\n\\nIf this was a mistake, ignore this email and nothing will happen.\\n\\nIn order to confirm this request, you can complete one of the following tasks:\\n\\n1. Visit the following URL: {TOKENURL}\\n\\n2. Copy your token from this email, visit the referenced URL, and paste your token into the form.\\nURL: {FORMURL}\\nToken: {TOKEN}\\n\\nPlease note that this token is only valid for 24 hours from the time this email was sent.","Seseorang telah membuat permintaan untuk mengeksport semua maklumat peribadi yang berkaitan dengan alamat e-mel ini di {URL}. Lawati URL berikut: {TOKENURL}\\n\\n2. Salin token anda daripada e-mel ini, lawati URL yang dirujuk, dan tampal token anda ke dalam borang.\\nURL: {FORMURL}\\nToken: {TOKEN}\\n\\nSila ambil perhatian bahawa token ini hanya sah selama 24 jam dari masa e-mel ini dihantar."],["com_privacy","COM_PRIVACY_EMAIL_REQUEST_BODY_REMOVE_REQUEST","Someone has created a request to remove all personal information related to this email address at {URL}. Visit the following URL: {TOKENURL}\\n\\n2. Lawati URL berikut: {TOKENURL}\\n\\n2. Setiap kod kecemasan ini akan dimusnahkan serta-merta selepas digunakan. Simpan atau muat semula untuk mengira semula kebenaran kumpulan ini."],["lib_joomla","JLIB_RULES_REQUEST_FAILURE","Failed sending the data to server.","Tidak berjaya menghantar data ke pelayan."],["lib_joomla","JLIB_RULES_SAVE_BEFORE_CHANGE_PERMISSIONS","Please save before changing permissions.","Sila simpan sebelum menukar kebenaran."],["lib_joomla","JLIB_RULES_SELECT_SETTING","Select New Setting","Pilih Tetapan Baharu"],["lib_joomla","JLIB_RULES_SETTING_NOTES","If you change the setting, it will apply to this and all child groups, components and content. Note that <em><strong>Denied<\/strong><\/em> will overrule any inherited setting and also the setting in any child group, component or content. In the case of a setting conflict, <em><strong>Deny<\/strong><\/em> will take precedence. <em><strong>Not Set<\/strong><\/em> is equivalent to <em><strong>Denied<\/strong><\/em> but can be changed in child groups, components and content.","Jika anda menukar tetapan, ia akan digunakan pada kumpulan ini dan semua kumpulan anak, komponen dan kandungan. Ambil perhatian bahawa <em><strong>Ditolak<\/strong><\/em> akan mengatasi sebarang tetapan yang diwarisi dan juga tetapan dalam mana-mana kumpulan anak, komponen atau kandungan. Jika berlaku konflik tetapan, <em><strong>Tolak<\/strong><\/em> akan diutamakan. <em><strong>Tidak Ditetapkan<\/strong><\/em> bersamaan dengan <em><strong>Ditolak<\/strong><\/em> tetapi boleh diubah dalam kumpulan anak, komponen dan kandungan."],["lib_joomla","JLIB_RULES_SETTING_NOTES_ITEM","If you change the setting, it will apply to this item. Note that:<br><em><strong>Inherited<\/strong><\/em> means that the permissions from global configuration, parent group and category will be used.<br><em><strong>Denied<\/strong><\/em> means that no matter what the global configuration, parent group or category settings are, the group being edited can't take this action on this item.<br><em><strong>Allowed<\/strong><\/em> means that the group being edited will be able to take this action for this item (but if this is in conflict with the global configuration, parent group or category it will have no impact; a conflict will be indicated by <em><strong>Not Allowed (Inherited)<\/strong><\/em> under Calculated Settings).","Jika anda menukar tetapan, ia akan digunakan pada item ini. Ambil perhatian bahawa:<br><em><strong>Diwarisi<\/strong><\/em> bermaksud kebenaran daripada tatarajah global, kumpulan induk dan kategori akan digunakan.<br><em><strong>Ditolak<\/strong><\/em> bermaksud walau apa pun tetapan tatarajah global, kumpulan induk atau kategori, kumpulan yang sedang disunting tidak boleh mengambil tindakan ini pada item ini.<br><em><strong>Dibenarkan<\/strong><\/em> bermaksud kumpulan yang sedang disunting akan dapat mengambil tindakan ini untuk item ini (tetapi jika ini bercanggah dengan tatarajah global, kumpulan induk atau kategori, ia tidak akan memberi kesan; konflik akan ditunjukkan dengan <em><strong>Tidak Dibenarkan (Diwarisi)<\/strong><\/em> di bawah Tetapan Terkira)."],["lib_joomla","JLIB_RULES_SETTINGS_DESC","Expand for notes about setting the permissions.","Kembangkan untuk nota tentang menetapkan kebenaran."],["lib_joomla","JLIB_STEMMER_INVALID_STEMMER","Invalid stemmer type %s","Jenis stemmer tidak sah %s"],["lib_joomla","JLIB_UNKNOWN","Unknown","Tidak Diketahui"],["lib_joomla","JLIB_UPDATER_ERROR_COLLECTION_FOPEN","The PHP allow_url_fopen setting is disabled. The preferred (most secure) hashing method supported by phpass is the OpenBSD-style bcrypt (known in PHP as CRYPT_BLOWFISH), with a fallback to BSDI-style extended DES-based hashes (known in PHP as CRYPT_EXT_DES) and a last resort fallback to an MD5-based variable iteration count password hashing method implemented in phpass itself.","phpass ialah rangka kerja pencincangan kata laluan mudah alih untuk digunakan dalam aplikasi PHP. If Normal Mode is chosen, then configure the module and it will display a static list of Articles on the menu items you assign the module to. If Dynamic Mode is chosen, then you can still configure the module normally, however now the Category option will no longer be used. Instead, the module will dynamically detect if you are on a Category view and will display the list of articles within that Category. When Dynamic Mode is chosen, it is best to leave the module set to display on all pages, as it will decide to display anything dynamically.","Sila pilih mod yang ingin anda gunakan. Jika Mod Normal dipilih, tatarajahkan modul dan ia akan memaparkan senarai Artikel yang statik pada item menu yang anda tetapkan modul ini. Jika Mod Dinamik dipilih, anda masih boleh menatarajah modul seperti biasa, tetapi pilihan Kategori tidak akan digunakan lagi. Sebaliknya, modul akan mengesan secara dinamik jika anda berada pada paparan Kategori dan akan memaparkan senarai artikel dalam Kategori tersebut. Apabila Mod Dinamik dipilih, adalah lebih baik membiarkan modul ditetapkan untuk dipaparkan pada semua halaman, kerana ia akan memutuskan untuk memaparkan sesuatu secara dinamik."],["mod_articles_category","MOD_ARTICLES_CATEGORY_FIELD_MODE_LABEL","Mode","Mod"],["mod_articles_category","MOD_ARTICLES_CATEGORY_FIELD_MONTHYEARFORMAT_DESC","Please enter in a valid date format. Berkaitan"],["mod_related_items","MOD_RELATED_XML_DESCRIPTION","This module displays other Articles that are related to the one being viewed. These relations are established by the Keywords. <br>All the keywords of the current Article are searched against all the keywords of all other published Articles. For example, you may have an Article on &quot;Breeding Parrots&quot; and another on &quot;Hand Raising Black Cockatoos&quot;. If you include the keyword &quot;parrot&quot; in both Articles, then the Related Items Module will list the &quot;Breeding Parrots&quot; Article when viewing &quot;Hand Raising Black Cockatoos&quot; and vice-versa.","Modul ini memaparkan Artikel lain yang berkaitan dengan artikel yang sedang dilihat. Perkaitan ini ditentukan oleh Kata Kunci. <br>Semua kata kunci Artikel semasa dicari berbanding semua kata kunci semua Artikel lain yang diterbitkan. Contohnya, anda mungkin mempunyai Artikel tentang &quot;Membiak Burung Kakak Tua&quot; dan satu lagi tentang &quot;Menjaga Kakak Tua Hitam dari Kecil&quot;. Jika anda memasukkan kata kunci &quot;kakak tua&quot; dalam kedua-dua Artikel, maka Modul Item Berkaitan akan menyenaraikan Artikel &quot;Membiak Burung Kakak Tua&quot; apabila melihat &quot;Menjaga Kakak Tua Hitam dari Kecil&quot; dan sebaliknya."],["mod_related_items.sys","MOD_RELATED_ITEMS","Articles - Related","Artikel - Artikel Berkaitan"],["mod_related_items.sys","MOD_RELATED_ITEMS_LAYOUT_DEFAULT","Default","Lalai"],["mod_related_items.sys","MOD_RELATED_XML_DESCRIPTION","This module displays other Articles that are related to the one being viewed. var PAGE=150,shown=0,list=D,term="";
var ta=document.createElement("textarea");
function dec(s){s=s.replace(/\\n/g,"\n").replace(/\\"/g,'"');ta.innerHTML=s;return ta.value}
function esc(s){return s.replace(/&/g,"&amp;").replace(/</g,"&lt;").replace(/>/g,"&gt;")}
function hl(s){var h=esc(s).replace(/\n/g,'<span class="nl">&crarr;</span>');
 if(!term)return h;
 var re=new RegExp("("+term.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,"\\$&")+")","gi");
 return h.replace(/(<[^>]*>)|([^<]+)/g,function(m,tag,txt){return tag?tag:txt.replace(re,"<mark>$1</mark>")})}
var tb=document.getElementById("tb"),more=document.getElementById("more"),cnt=document.getElementById("cnt"),emp=document.getElementById("emp");
var fsel=document.getElementById("f");
var files={};D.forEach(function(r){files[r[0]]=(files[r[0]]||0)+1});
fsel.innerHTML='<option value="">Semua fail ('+D.length+')</option>'+Object.keys(files).map(function(k){return '<option value="'+k+'">'+k+' ('+files[k]+')</option>'}).join("");
function row(r){return '<tr><td class="k"><b>'+esc(r[0])+'</b>'+hl(r[1])+'</td><td class="en">'+hl(dec(r[2]))+'</td><td class="ms">'+hl(dec(r[3]))+'</td></tr>'}
function render(reset){
 if(reset){tb.innerHTML="";shown=0}
 var end=Math.min(shown+PAGE,list.length),h="";
 for(var i=shown;i<end;i++)h+=row(list[i]);
 tb.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend",h);shown=end;
 more.hidden=shown>=list.length;emp.hidden=list.length>0;
 cnt.textContent=list.length.toLocaleString()+" rentetan"}
function apply(){
 var f=fsel.value;term=document.getElementById("q").value.trim();var t=term.toLowerCase();
 list=D.filter(function(r){return(!f||r[0]===f)&&(!t||r[1].toLowerCase().indexOf(t)>-1||dec(r[2]).toLowerCase().indexOf(t)>-1||dec(r[3]).toLowerCase().indexOf(t)>-1)});
 render(true)}
var to;document.getElementById("q").addEventListener("input",function(){clearTimeout(to);to=setTimeout(apply,180)});
fsel.addEventListener("change",apply);more.addEventListener("click",function(){render(false)});
function mode(m){document.getElementById("t").classList.toggle("hide-en",m);
 document.getElementById("b1").setAttribute("aria-pressed",!m);document.getElementById("b2").setAttribute("aria-pressed",m)}
document.getElementById("b1").onclick=function(){mode(false)};document.getElementById("b2").onclick=function(){mode(true)};
render(true);
</script>
</body>
</html>
